**Integration Points Between Strategic and Tactical Dashboards:**

| Strategic Dashboard | Feeds Into | Key Data Elements |
|---------------------|------------|-------------------|
| F.14 Geopolitical | F.17 Synthesis, F.9 Scenario | Global stability index, flashpoint probabilities, megatrends |
| F.15 Political | F.17 Synthesis, F.4 Threat | Polarization index, policy trajectories, extremism levels |
| F.16 Alliance/Adversary | F.4 Threat, F.7 COG | Actor relationships, coalition dynamics, funding flows |
| F.17 Strategic Synthesis | F.1 Executive, F.9 Scenario | Causal chains, cross-level correlations, strategic assessment |

**Feed Type Classification:**

| Feed Type | Description | Use Case | Latency |
|-----------|-------------|----------|---------|
| **WebSocket/SSE** | Persistent connection, server-push | Real-time alerts, market data, social streams | <1 sec |
| **REST API Polling** | Client-initiated requests at intervals | Scheduled data updates, status checks | Seconds-minutes |
| **Webhook** | Server-initiated HTTP callbacks | Event notifications, trigger-based updates | <5 sec |
| **RSS/Atom** | Structured syndication feeds | News, blog, publication updates | Minutes-hours |
| **GraphQL** | Flexible query-based retrieval | Complex data relationships, social graphs | Seconds |
| **FTP/SFTP** | Bulk file transfer | Data dumps, scheduled reports | Hours |
| **Message Queue** | Pub/sub messaging (AMQP, Kafka) | High-volume event streams, microservice communication | Milliseconds |
| **Manual Entry** | Human-entered data | HUMINT, assessments, ratings | On-demand |

**Source Reliability Tiers:**

| Tier | Definition | Validation Required | Examples |
|------|------------|-------------------|----------|
| **Authoritative** | Official source with legal/regulatory standing | None | Government databases, SEC filings, court records |
| **Validated** | Third-party verified or multi-source corroborated | Cross-reference | Wire services, established media, academic peer-reviewed |
| **Semi-Validated** | Single reliable source, not independently verified | Flag confidence | Industry publications, reputable blogs, named sources |
| **Unvalidated** | Raw collection, accuracy unknown | Require corroboration | Social media posts, anonymous tips, scraped content |
| **User-Contributed** | Analyst/user-entered assessments | Peer review | Analyst ratings, manual observations, HUMINT |

#### F.19.5 Data Quality & Reliability Framework

```
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                              DATA QUALITY ASSURANCE FRAMEWORK                                    │
├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│                                                                                                  │
│  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐│
│  │   SOURCE RELIABILITY RATING (Admiralty Scale Mapping)                                       ││
│  │                                                                                             ││
│  │   ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ ││
│  │   │  SOURCE RELIABILITY          │  INFORMATION ACCURACY                                 │ ││
│  │   ├──────────────────────────────┼───────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤ ││
│  │   │  A - Completely Reliable     │  1 - Confirmed by other independent sources          │ ││
│  │   │      (Official/Authoritative)│      (Multiple corroboration)                        │ ││
│  │   │                              │                                                       │ ││
│  │   │  B - Usually Reliable        │  2 - Probably true                                   │ ││
│  │   │      (Validated track record)│      (Logical, consistent with known facts)          │ ││
│  │   │                              │                                                       │ ││
│  │   │  C - Fairly Reliable         │  3 - Possibly true                                   │ ││
│  │   │      (Some validation)       │      (Possible but not confirmed)                    │ ││
│  │   │                              │                                                       │ ││
│  │   │  D - Not Usually Reliable    │  4 - Doubtful                                        │ ││
│  │   │      (Limited track record)  │      (Questionable, contradictory)                   │ ││
│  │   │                              │                                                       │ ││
│  │   │  E - Unreliable              │  5 - Improbable                                      │ ││
│  │   │      (Poor track record)     │      (Unlikely, contradicts known facts)             │ ││
│  │   │                              │                                                       │ ││
│  │   │  F - Cannot Be Judged        │  6 - Truth cannot be judged                          │ ││
│  │   │      (New/unknown source)    │      (No basis for evaluation)                       │ ││
│  │   └──────────────────────────────┴───────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ ││
│  │                                                                                             ││
│  │   Example Rating: "B2" = Usually reliable source, information probably true               ││
│  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘│
│                                                                                                  │
│  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐│
│  │   DATA FRESHNESS INDICATORS                                                                 ││
│  │                                                                                             ││
│  │   ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ ││
│  │   │  Freshness Level    │  Time Since Update  │  Visual Indicator  │  Action Required   │ ││
│  │   ├─────────────────────┼─────────────────────┼────────────────────┼────────────────────┤ ││
│  │   │  🟢 CURRENT         │  < Expected refresh │  Green dot         │  None              │ ││
│  │   │  🟡 AGING           │  1-2x expected      │  Yellow dot        │  Monitor           │ ││
│  │   │  🟠 STALE           │  2-5x expected      │  Orange dot        │  Investigate       │ ││
│  │   │  🔴 EXPIRED         │  > 5x expected      │  Red dot           │  Mark unreliable   │ ││
│  │   │  ⚫ UNKNOWN         │  No timestamp       │  Gray dot          │  Validate          │ ││
│  │   └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ ││
│  │                                                                                             ││
│  │   Dashboard Display: Each data element shows last update time + freshness indicator        ││
│  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘│
│                                                                                                  │
│  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐│
│  │   CONFIDENCE SCORING                                                                        ││
│  │                                                                                             ││
│  │   ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ ││
│  │   │                                                                                      │ ││
│  │   │  CONFIDENCE = f(Source Reliability, Corroboration, Recency, Consistency)            │ ││
│  │   │                                                                                      │ ││
│  │   │  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐    │ ││
│  │   │  │  Factor              │ Weight │ Scoring                                     │    │ ││
│  │   │  ├──────────────────────┼────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────┤    │ ││
│  │   │  │  Source Reliability  │  30%   │ A=100, B=80, C=60, D=40, E=20, F=10        │    │ ││
│  │   │  │  Corroboration       │  30%   │ 3+ sources=100, 2=75, 1=50, 0=25           │    │ ││
│  │   │  │  Recency             │  20%   │ Current=100, Aging=75, Stale=50, Exp=25    │    │ ││
│  │   │  │  Consistency         │  20%   │ Matches patterns=100, Neutral=75, Anomaly=50│    │ ││
│  │   │  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘    │ ││
│  │   │                                                                                      │ ││
│  │   │  Confidence Levels:  HIGH (>80)  │  MODERATE (60-80)  │  LOW (<60)                  │ ││
│  │   │                                                                                      │ ││
│  │   └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ ││
│  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘│
│                                                                                                  │
│  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐│
│  │   CORROBORATION REQUIREMENTS                                                                ││
│  │                                                                                             ││
│  │   ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ ││
│  │   │  Information Type           │ Min Sources │ Source Diversity Required               │ ││
│  │   ├─────────────────────────────┼─────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────────┤ ││
│  │   │  Factual claim (verifiable) │      2      │ Different source types                  │ ││
│  │   │  Assessment/opinion         │      1      │ Analyst qualification required          │ ││
│  │   │  Threat warning             │      2      │ Independent confirmation                │ ││
│  │   │  Intent attribution         │      3      │ HUMINT + OSINT + behavioral            │ ││
│  │   │  Capability claim           │      2      │ Technical validation                    │ ││
│  │   │  Timeline/event prediction  │      2      │ Different methodologies                 │ ││
│  │   │  Relationship/network claim │      2      │ Different evidence types                │ ││
│  │   └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ ││
│  │                                                                                             ││
│  │   Single-Source Flagging: All single-source claims marked with ⚠ indicator                ││
│  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘│
│                                                                                                  │
│  ┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐│
│  │   AUTOMATED VALIDATION RULES                                                                ││
│  │                                                                                             ││
│  │   ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐ ││
│  │   │  Rule Type            │ Implementation                                               │ ││
│  │   ├───────────────────────┼──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤ ││
│  │   │  Schema validation    │ JSON Schema / data type enforcement                         │ ││
│  │   │  Range checks         │ Numeric values within expected bounds                       │ ││
│  │   │  Temporal logic       │ Dates/times in valid sequence                               │ ││
│  │   │  Entity resolution    │ Deduplication and canonical linking                         │ ││
│  │   │  Geographic validation│ Coordinates within expected region                          │ ││
│  │   │  Cross-reference      │ Foreign keys exist in related datasets                      │ ││
│  │   │  Anomaly detection    │ Statistical outlier flagging                                │ ││
│  │   │  Completeness check   │ Required fields populated                                   │ ││
│  │   │  Duplicate detection  │ Near-duplicate content identification                       │ ││
│  │   │  Source verification  │ API response validation, signature checks                   │ ││
│  │   └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘ ││
│  └─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘│
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```
